EXPIRY: because Request has no expiry field the if-it-expires branch HAS NO MODEL ANTECEDENT, so there is no modelled lapse and no modelled recovery-on-expiry path to establish; the requester exit is the existing withdraw path with refund to r.proposal.refundAddress, exact and per request.
